{"forms":[{"form":"trophies","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"He won the trophy in a running competition.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An object, usually in the form of a statuette, cup, or shield, awarded for success in a competition or to mark a special achievement."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The set of antlers which hung on the wall was his prized trophy.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An object taken as a prize by a hunter, or a conqueror or belligerent, especially one that is displayed."]},{"examples":[{"text":"His trophies included his second wife, his successful children, the third and fourth homes in Palm Beach and Malibu, and his three yachts.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any emblem of success; a status symbol."]},{"glosses":["An object taken by a serial killer or rapist as a memento of the crime."],"tags":["broadly"],"topics":["criminology","human-sciences","law","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A tropæum."],"tags":["Ancient-Rome","historical"]},{"glosses":["A display of weaponry and other militaria, often captured from a defeated enemy, as an ornament designed for the purpose of triumphalist display by a victor or as a show of military prowess by a monarch."],"topics":["architecture","art","arts"]},{"glosses":["An artifact or artwork that has been stolen by a criminal and traded on the black market."]},{"glosses":["An animal killed by a trophy hunter that usually has its parts sold on the black market."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈtɹəʊfi/","tags":["UK"]},{"ipa":"/ˈtɹɒfi/","tags":["UK","obsolete"]},{"ipa":"/ˈtɹoʊfi/","tags":["US"]}],"upstream_line":374389,"upstream_offset":1802648127,"upstream_record_sha256":"a9e6df5f26792b177ff505ecb77ce66eea7a4b9f43ad33972248343145bf17fc","word":"trophy"}

{"forms":[{"form":"whereever","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"conj","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Wherever you go, I’ll find you.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Regardless of the place in, at or to which."]},{"examples":[{"text":"You can sit wherever you like.","type":"example"},{"text":"I'd follow you wherever you went.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In, at or to any place (that); anywhere."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Add quotations wherever they are needed.","type":"example"},{"text":"I see mistakes wherever I look.","type":"example"},{"text":"Our charity has limited funds, but we help wherever we can.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In, at or to all places or situations (that); everywhere."]},{"examples":[{"text":"She lives in Puddletown, wherever that is.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to show that one does not know where someone or something is located."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/(h)wɛɹˈɛvɚ/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/wɛəˈɹɛvə/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]}],"upstream_line":288997,"upstream_offset":1441841915,"upstream_record_sha256":"ca01a081e8747201b475a20344e42ac96ef4b70620df0653ac120fde82d68f44","word":"wherever"}
